# Sakuya Cheat Menu Pro - Block 3 Amendment Scripted GUIs
sakuya_b3_amendment_political_concession_current_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_add_political_concession_default = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	is_valid = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { enacting_any_law = yes exists = currently_enacting_law }
			sakuya_b3_amendment_save_selected_ig_effect = yes
			currently_enacting_law = {
				if = {
					limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b3_amendment_sponsor }
					add_amendment = {
						type = amendment_generic_sponsor_polstr_increase
						sponsor = scope:sakuya_b3_amendment_sponsor
						cooldown = 120
					}
				}
				else = {
					sakuya_b3_amendment_add_to_law_selected_sponsor_effect = { TYPE = amendment_generic_sponsor_polstr_increase }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_add_political_concession_right = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	is_valid = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { enacting_any_law = yes exists = currently_enacting_law }
			sakuya_b3_amendment_save_selected_ig_effect = yes
			currently_enacting_law = {
				if = {
					limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b3_amendment_sponsor }
					add_amendment = {
						type = amendment_generic_sponsor_polstr_approval_increase
						sponsor = scope:sakuya_b3_amendment_sponsor
						cooldown = 120
					}
				}
				else = {
					sakuya_b3_amendment_add_to_law_selected_sponsor_effect = { TYPE = amendment_generic_sponsor_polstr_approval_increase }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_remove_political_concession_current = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	is_valid = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { enacting_any_law = yes exists = currently_enacting_law }
			currently_enacting_law = {
				every_scope_amendment = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							type = amendment_type:amendment_generic_sponsor_polstr_increase
							type = amendment_type:amendment_generic_sponsor_polstr_approval_increase
							type = amendment_type:amendment_generic_sponsor_polstr_increase_negotiation_1
							type = amendment_type:amendment_generic_sponsor_polstr_increase_negotiation_2
							type = amendment_type:amendment_generic_sponsor_polstr_increase_negotiation_3
						}
					}
					remove_amendment = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_add_political_concession_shift = {

sakuya_b3_amendment_active_check_amendment_geheime_staatskonferenz_metternich = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_current_check_amendment_geheime_staatskonferenz_metternich = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		is_player = yes
		enacting_any_law = yes
			exists = currently_enacting_law
			OR = {
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_autocracy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_bakufu
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_neo_absolutism
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_technocracy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_organic_regulation
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_elder_council
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_landed_voting
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_anarchy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_single_party_state
			}
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_add_active_amendment_geheime_staatskonferenz_metternich = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	is_valid = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
			active_law:lawgroup_distribution_of_power ?= {
				sakuya_b3_amendment_add_to_law_selected_sponsor_effect = { TYPE = amendment_geheime_staatskonferenz_metternich }
			}
	}
}

	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	is_valid = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
			enacting_any_law = yes
			exists = currently_enacting_law
			OR = {
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_chiefdom
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_monarchy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_colonial_administration
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_presidential_republic
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_parliamentary_republic
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_theocracy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_council_republic
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporate_state
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_social_monarchy
			}
		}
			currently_enacting_law = {
				sakuya_b3_amendment_add_to_law_selected_sponsor_effect = { TYPE = amendment_corporate_state_nominating_body_id }
			}
		}
	}

	is_shown = { is_player = yes }

			limit = {
			enacting_any_law = yes

							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_monarchy

sakuya_b3_amendment_current_check_amendment_racialized_subjecthood = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		is_player = yes
		enacting_any_law = yes
			exists = currently_enacting_law
			OR = {
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_subjecthood
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_ethnostate
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_national_supremacy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_racial_segregation
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_cultural_exclusion
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_multicultural
			}
	}
}

# Sakuya generated amendment availability/remove helpers start
sakuya_b3_amendment_active_has_amendment_geheime_staatskonferenz_metternich = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		is_player = yes
		OR = {
			active_law:lawgroup_distribution_of_power ?= {
				OR = {
					has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_geheime_staatskonferenz_metternich
					has_amendment = amendment_type:sakuya_amendment_geheime_staatskonferenz_metternich
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_active_available_amendment_geheime_staatskonferenz_metternich = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		is_player = yes
		NOT = { sakuya_b3_amendment_active_has_amendment_geheime_staatskonferenz_metternich = yes }
		OR = {
			has_law = law_type:law_autocracy
		}
		c:AUS ?= this
				has_law = law_type:law_autocracy # Not neo-absolutism
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_remove_active_amendment_geheime_staatskonferenz_metternich = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	is_valid = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		active_law:lawgroup_distribution_of_power ?= {
			every_scope_amendment = {
				remove_amendment = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_remove_current_amendment_geheime_staatskonferenz_metternich = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	is_valid = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { enacting_any_law = yes exists = currently_enacting_law }
			currently_enacting_law = {
				every_scope_amendment = {
					remove_amendment = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_active_available_amendment_economic_regeneration_1 = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		is_player = yes
		NOT = { sakuya_b3_amendment_active_has_amendment_economic_regeneration_1 = yes }
		OR = {
			has_law = law_type:law_traditionalism
			has_law = law_type:law_agrarianism
			has_law = law_type:law_interventionism
			has_law = law_type:law_industry_banned
			has_law = law_type:law_laissez_faire
			has_law = law_type:law_cooperative_ownership
			has_law = law_type:law_command_economy
			has_law = law_type:law_extraction_economy
		}
		OR = {
					c:SPA ?= this
					c:POR ?= this
					was_formed_from = SPA
					was_formed_from = POR
				}
				OR = {
					has_variable = je_spa_economic_regeneration_master_completed_var
					has_variable = regeneration_complete
				}
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_remove_current_amendment_open_legal_questions = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	is_valid = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { enacting_any_law = yes exists = currently_enacting_law }
			currently_enacting_law = {
				every_scope_amendment = {
					remove_amendment = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		OR = {
			has_law = law_type:law_tenant_farmers

			has_law = law_type:law_homesteading
			has_law = law_type:law_peasant_proprietorship

		NOR = {

		is_player = yes
		NOT = { sakuya_b3_amendment_active_has_amendment_secular_school_system = yes }
		OR = {
			has_law = law_type:law_public_schools
		}
		always = yes
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_remove_active_amendment_secular_school_system = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	is_valid = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		active_law:lawgroup_education_system ?= {
			every_scope_amendment = {
				remove_amendment = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

			}
		}
	}

			has_law = law_type:law_traditionalism
			has_law = law_type:law_agrarianism
			has_law = law_type:law_interventionism
			has_law = law_type:law_industry_banned
			has_law = law_type:law_laissez_faire
			has_law = law_type:law_cooperative_ownership
			has_law = law_type:law_command_economy
			has_law = law_type:law_extraction_economy
		}
		OR = {
					c:JAP ?= this
					was_formed_from = JAP
				}
				has_variable = zaibatsu_victory_var
				NOT = {
					any_active_law = {
						OR = {
							has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_zaibatsu_oligopoly_2
							has_amendment = amendment_type:sakuya_amendment_zaibatsu_oligopoly_2
						}

	}

sakuya_b3_amendment_add_current_sakuya_amendment_cult_of_personality = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	is_valid = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
			enacting_any_law = yes
			exists = currently_enacting_law
			OR = {
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_autocracy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_bakufu
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_neo_absolutism
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_technocracy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_organic_regulation
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_elder_council
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_landed_voting
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_anarchy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_single_party_state
			}
		}
			currently_enacting_law = {
				sakuya_b3_amendment_add_to_law_selected_sponsor_effect = { TYPE = sakuya_amendment_cult_of_personality }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_add_active_sakuya_amendment_farmers_cooperatives = {

		}
	}

							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_anarchy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_single_party_state
			}
		}
			currently_enacting_law = {
				sakuya_b3_amendment_add_to_law_selected_sponsor_effect = { TYPE = sakuya_amendment_electoral_clientelism }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_add_active_sakuya_amendment_tradition_of_free_elections = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	is_valid = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		active_law:lawgroup_distribution_of_power ?= {
			sakuya_b3_amendment_add_to_law_selected_sponsor_effect = { TYPE = sakuya_amendment_tradition_of_free_elections }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b3_amendment_add_current_sakuya_amendment_tradition_of_free_elections = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { is_player = yes }
	is_valid = { is_player = yes }
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
			enacting_any_law = yes
			exists = currently_enacting_law
			OR = {
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_autocracy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_bakufu
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_neo_absolutism
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_technocracy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_organic_regulation
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_elder_council
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_landed_voting
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_anarchy
							is_enacting_law = law_type:law_single_party_state
			}
		}
			currently_enacting_law = {
				sakuya_b3_amendment_add_to_law_selected_sponsor_effect = { TYPE = sakuya_amendment_tradition_of_free_elections }
			}
		}
	}
}
